- **spx:agent-context**: Refresh the managed Spec Kit section in the coding agent context file (CLAUDE.md) to point at the current plan, and refresh the managed codex review-guidelines block in AGENTS.md. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/agent-context.md))
- **spx:analyze**: Non-destructive cross-artifact consistency and quality analysis across spec.md, plan.md, and tasks.md after task generation, grounded by an Elixir/Phoenix specialist pass. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/analyze.md))
- **spx:assess**: Pre-specify idea pipeline. Run one of five phases — intake, research, define, shape, decide — under .specify/assessments/&lt;slug&gt;/. The decide gate returns go / needs-clarification / kill; a go hands off to /spx:specify. Killing weak ideas here is the point. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/assess.md))
- **spx:bug**: Artifact-based bugfix loop. Run one of three phases — assess, fix, test — under .specify/bugs/&lt;slug&gt;/. Assess delegates to the investigate machinery (root-cause + Tidewave capture); only the fix phase edits source; test verifies the fix holds without regressions. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/bug.md))
- **spx:checklist**: Generate a custom requirements-quality checklist ("unit tests for English") for the current feature, with optional Phoenix domain presets (security, deploy, oban, boundaries). ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/checklist.md))
- **spx:clarify**: Identify underspecified areas in the current feature spec by asking up to 5 highly targeted clarification questions, scored across coverage dimensions, and encoding the answers back into the spec. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/clarify.md))
- **spx:constitution**: Create or update the project constitution (the canonical Iron-Law corpus) from interactive or provided principle inputs, regenerating every derived enforcement copy and keeping dependent templates in sync. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/constitution.md))
- **spx:converge**: Post-implement gap assessment. Assess the codebase against the feature's spec, plan, and tasks, then append any remaining unbuilt work as a new append-only Convergence phase to tasks.md so /spx:implement can complete it. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/converge.md))
- **spx:doctor**: Read-only health report for the spec-driven workspace. Checks the .specify/ state tree, feature.json validity, constitution presence and derived-copy freshness, hook wiring, tool availability (mix, gh, jq, codex, rtk), and probes Tidewave. Never modifies anything. Pass --json for machine-readable output. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/doctor.md))
- **spx:full**: Autonomous driver over the WHOLE spec-driven chain — specify, clarify, plan, tasks, analyze, checklist, implement, verify, review, fix cycles, converge, compound. State machine in progress.md, cycle caps, optional human gates. Use for large features or end-to-end autonomous delivery. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/full.md))

- **spx:git-feature**: Create a feature branch with sequential or timestamp numbering ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/git-feature.md))
- **spx:git-init**: Initialize a Git repository with an initial commit ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/git-init.md))
- **spx:git-remote**: Detect Git remote URL for GitHub integration ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/git-remote.md))

- **spx:implement**: Execute the implementation plan by processing all tasks in tasks.md — task-loop engine with per-task verification, 3-retry BLOCKERs, an Elixir/Phoenix Verification Gate, review-triage, and a specific-file git protocol. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/implement.md))
- **spx:init**: Bootstrap the spec-driven workspace in this project. Creates the .specify/ tree and cross-feature state dirs, copies templates, records feature-numbering options, detects the stack, installs the CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md marker blocks, and seeds the constitution ONLY if missing. Idempotent and customization-preserving — never overwrites your edits. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/init.md))
- **spx:plan**: Execute the implementation planning workflow — research fan-out via specialist agents, then generate design artifacts and a phased, agent-routed plan with an Elixir/Phoenix Design section. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/plan.md))
- **spx:specify**: Create or update the feature specification from a natural language feature description, seeding the feature workspace and consuming any brainstorm interview. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/specify.md))
- **spx:tasks**: Generate an actionable, dependency-ordered tasks.md for the feature from the design artifacts, with Elixir/Phoenix task rules, [agent] routing tags, and migration sequencing. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/tasks.md))
- **spx:tasks-to-issues**: Convert existing tasks in tasks.md into actionable, dependency-ordered GitHub issues for the feature, deduplicated against existing issues. gh CLI primary, GitHub MCP fallback. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/tasks-to-issues.md))
